# Source-derived material: tip inventory and artifact audit

**Feasibility track T8, 2026-11-28. Proposal only; not legal advice or release clearance.**
Baseline: local `origin/main` `6d2de9f4d751003b0d07e037084bd3788c4ba021`, 5,069 tracked paths.
No data file was moved, deleted or modified. No history or repository settings were changed.

## Scope and reproducibility

Based on the existing [rights memo](plans/2026-11-25-rights-and-provenance-memo.md),
[separation design](plans/2026-11-25-content-pack-separation-design.md), README and NOTICE.
The inventory enumerates every path flagged by the baseline guard, including known carve-outs,
archives, review packets, potential literals, provenance references and shape-only hits.
The guard does not establish that a candidate reproduces protected expression or that an unflagged
file is data-free. Unmarked paraphrases, small literals and data in arbitrary encodings require
manual review. In particular, the broad plan/fixture path rules include original and synthetic
material; do not treat their inclusion as a legal finding. All uncertain rows are explicitly candidates.

Run from the repository root:

```sh
python3 scripts/source_derived_guard.py
python3 scripts/source_derived_guard.py --strict
python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts -p test_source_derived_guard.py
```

The deterministic JSON report contains only paths, status, and detector counts. Default report-only
exits zero on findings; strict mode exits one for unexpected hits or stale manifest paths. Invalid
manifest / Git failures are operational errors. Allow-list entries are exact paths with reasons, not
globs or legal authorization. SHA-256 hashes pin allowed content; changes to matched paths and new
matching paths are unexpected. The manifest is the sole path-only exception (a self-hash is impossible);
review its diffs explicitly. Missing tracked paths are unexpected even if allow-listed. Shrink the
manifest as separation proceeds.

Detection includes source identifiers/symbols, source-origin and locator references, provenance
language, known path families, JSON/JSONL of at least 16 KiB with unit/hex identifier keys, numeric
grids (four contiguous rows, six or more cells each, eight distinct values, entropy >= 2.5 bits),
comma-separated runs of 32 literal tokens, and all archive suffixes. Files over 2 MiB and opaque
non-UTF-8 files are conservatively flagged without detailed analysis; archives are not unpacked.
These bounds are heuristic, not a proof of absence. Only the metadata report is uploaded by the
observational PR workflow; no content or binary output is uploaded by this guard.

## Public-history exposure

The repository is public. The listed paths existed at the pinned main tip, so their data or references
were already available through Git. First-add column records the earliest reachable addition under
the exact path found by `git log --diff-filter=A --no-renames --format=... --name-only -z origin/main`.
It does not follow renames or prove when a specific value first appeared. As a result, first-add
is a path-level history hint, not a complete exposure timeline. A tip-only cleanup does not remove
older commits, forks, clones, cached copies, or previously distributed artifacts. Counsel and owner
must separately decide preservation and any history remediation; none is proposed for execution here.

## Local Core binary measurement

Built the baseline locally using `dotnet build src/Cna.Core/Cna.Core.csproj -c Release --nologo`
(.NET SDK 10.0.400; exit 0, zero warnings/errors). Inspected the resulting assembly via
`Assembly.LoadFrom`, `GetManifestResourceNames` and `GetManifestResourceStream`, recording
stream lengths only. The Core build is sufficient to demonstrate the resource payload
that a normal Host publish referencing Core would include; Host publish itself was not run.

- Assembly: `artifacts/bin/Cna.Core/release/Cna.Core.dll`, 7,918,592 bytes.
- Assembly SHA-256: `bc07020bf30d3c0d6844ff9a9dca8800e5a0153876aae73a21e240169cf8f805`.
- 33 manifest resources, 1,668,581 bytes total uncompressed stream lengths.
- Confirmed source-derived map resources: 2, 1,375,448 bytes total.
- Other resources: the synthetic rules-lab family and linked canonical fixture per the separation
  memo. Sidecars without independent origin labels belong to that fixture family; verify their
  provenance before release. Resource names alone are not proof of synthetic provenance.
- Source literals in C# are also compiled into the assembly (TABLE and MIXED rows below); the
  reflection measurement does not isolate their IL or metadata sizes. Source file bytes in the
  inventory are **not** their compiled sizes.

Repeat the measurement locally with a temporary .NET 10 console project containing:

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
var assembly = Assembly.LoadFrom(Path.GetFullPath(args[0]));
foreach (var name in assembly.GetManifestResourceNames().Order(StringComparer.Ordinal))
{
    using var stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(name)!;
    Console.WriteLine($"{name}\t{stream.Length}");
}
```

Pass the built DLL path as the first argument. Do not upload the DLL or resource streams.

## Candidate inventory

2,197 distinct paths at the pinned tip (one row per path). Bytes are exact tracked
working-tree file sizes at the unmodified baseline. Category codes define kind, current artifact
route and proposed per-path migration target; all routes describing docs and tests refer to
normal Core/Host production builds, not historical CI packages or arbitrary user builds.
Unknown categories deliberately default to review/private storage where appropriate.

| Code | Kind | Current route | Proposed target | Paths |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | ---: |
| MAP | Established map source data | Core manifest resource | Private pack | 2 |
| TABLE | Established source table/roster or mixed implementation | Core compiled C# | Private pack values; retain original code | 9 |
| MIXED | Source-track code; data/reference distinction unresolved | Core compiled C# | Private pack values; retain original code | 105 |
| TEST | Source-related test or shape candidate | Test assembly/fixture only | Private conformance or synthetic replacement | 114 |
| GOLDEN | Established source golden / excerpt | Test assembly/fixture only | Private conformance | 5 |
| PACKET | Review packet or source-plan candidate | Tracked review packet/research | Private store pending review | 1294 |
| EVIDENCE | Archive or evidence candidate | Tracked archive/evidence | Private store pending review | 240 |
| FIXTURE | Source or mixed fixture candidate | Docs fixture (not Core resource) | Private store pending review | 89 |
| SYNTH | Synthetic fixture family per separation memo | Core manifest resource | Keep as public; verify provenance | 1 |
| CODE | Marker or shape candidate; may be reference-only | Compiled code or build input; review project | Keep public original code; extract any data privately | 126 |
| DOC | Provenance, rule-note or shape candidate | Tracked docs/metadata; not Core resource | Private store pending review; retain cleared prose | 202 |
| META | Original provenance/engineering guidance | Tracked docs/tooling; not data payload | Keep as public; review for quoted values | 10 |

## Follow-up proposal

1. Owner and counsel review the inventory and [questions](2026-11-counsel-questions.md);
   confirm the private boundary and separate original mechanics from source material.
2. Implement content-pack separation (#627) and synthetic fixture replacement without
   redistributing real packs. Keep original schemas and engineering code public.
3. Migrate reviewed data/evidence at the tip in a separate authorized change, shrink the
   allow-list, and verify production builds contain neither source resources nor literals.
4. Only after these checks and distribution clearance, consider strict CI and public release.
   No history rewrite or data deletion is included in T8.
